#### 48.3.1. Identity versus represented records and domain scope

The adopted document identity meaning is qualified Domain+Identifier;normalized logical identity is separate from exact edition,mutable classification,path,Status,maturity and membership. DF-SSS-NNN is the adopted corpus representation with stable allocation namespaces,not an identifier format imposed on every external domain or recalculated from Family/Type. Domain qualification matters to uniqueness;no global bare-string guarantee or implicit domain registration is inferred. Preserve valid identities through ordinary evolution and never reuse retired allocations.

Functional-file exclusion applies at all locations before normalized ID obligations. Template definitions/instances and graph/relationship endpoint kinds have their own qualified contracts;normalized Template documents can use document IDs without giving every Template entity or edge a DF document ID. Relationships owns typed connection records;Document Identity owns document endpoint identity semantics. Registry/Graph projections and identity use preserve source/edition provenance without becoming competing semantic owners or obligatory intermediaries.

Necessary schema/ID migration is already authorized when justified,but must have actual previous/new mappings,collision/reference/history/context/edition and consumer checks before coordinated application. Validity is not permission for arbitrary reassignment;immutable snapshots retain exact historical selections and mappings. No migration or allocation is needed for this localized source UPDATE. Historical reference preservation does not guarantee currently available content or a deployed resolver.

### 49.3. Actual identity contracts, pending peer discrepancies and maps

#### 49.3.1. Normalized identity, domain, edition and migration boundaries

All-location functional exclusions precede normalized ID/metadata obligations while preserving content/reference/organization/history/hash checks. Logical documents,exact editions,physical files,descriptors,Template definitions/instances and graph/relationship endpoint keys remain distinct. Normalized Template documentation can need document identity without assigning DF document IDs to every Template entity or edge.

Domain+Identifier qualifies logical identity and uniqueness. The adopted corpus representation DF-SSS-NNN uses stable registered three-letter allocation series and 001–999 positive numbers,independent of current Family/Type/Component/path/Version/Revision/Status. Equal suffixes across series are not collisions;000 is not allocated,gaps do not prove availability and allocated/retired numbers are not reused. New series/allocation/continuation needs records/collision/history evidence;no external domain syntax or allocation is imposed here. Detailed DID schema/constraint/specification integration remains pending.

Valid assigned identity remains associated with the same document during normal evolution. Immutable key under its applicable contract differs from mutable state/reference/location/descriptor/edition records. Necessary authorized schema/ID migration first assesses and reconciles governing previous/new rules with identity/domain owners,then records old→new mapping,same-document continuity,aliases/reservations,collisions,history/reference/context/edition/consumer effects and transition checks before coordinated application. Historical representations/snapshot members remain pinned;arbitrary reassignment,retired-ID reuse and simultaneous contradictory rules are not permitted. No migration occurs in this UPDATE.

Unassigned describes a pre-allocation subject;identity-record subjects/states and document Status/maturity/edition/snapshot membership are separate. Retirement preserves reservation/history/reference interpretation without guaranteeing a resolver or retrieved content. Exact edition references need coordinates/bytes/context beyond logical identity;identity alone selects no Baseline member. DMS/tool/manual operations supply allocation/lookup/persistence/transition evidence;CMS change scope does not grant documentary approval.

Logical identity belongs to the same document under its domain/allocation contract and persists across ordinary content/classification/location/edition changes. Stable corpus DF-SSS-NNN allocation series do not reconstruct identity from current Family/Type. Domain qualification is needed to interpret uniqueness;other-domain use needs actual qualified owner requirements rather than silent registration or external syntax imposition. Identifier is a representation,not the entity itself;detailed schema/constraint/specification integration remains pending.

Lifetime needs active versus historical subject distinction. Ending active use/deleting or retiring a document must not permit reassigning its allocated identity to another document or erasing retained mappings/reservations. Valid historical references do not guarantee a running resolver or retrievable content. Necessary authorized schema/ID migration first explicitly reconciles governing previous/new contracts,old-new/same-document/alias/reservation/collision/history/edition/consumer and transition evidence. Normal identity preservation and immutable snapshot history remain obligations;no arbitrary reassignment or actual migration is performed here.

Document identity is separate from exact document edition/coordinates/Status,maturity,identity-record state,Template/Metadata entity keys and relationship/graph-edge identities. Functional-file exclusions apply at every location with content/reference/history checks retained. A pre-allocation subject is not an assigned key;a retired record is not evidence of current publication or content availability. ## 54. User amendment — Cumulative documentary authorship

### 54.1. Adopted rule and attribution scope

On2026-10-14,the user requested that Author list the authors involved in each document,separated by a comma or semicolon. The working convention uses **semicolon followed by a space (`; `)**. Document headers list all evidenced original and later authors in first known contribution order,without duplicate names. Existing role qualifications may remain. Local edition histories identify actual contributions separately;the cumulative list does not assert every author performed every event.

DF-RVW-001 §7.1 now owns this convention. Preserve evidenced original authors in successors;add later authors when actual contribution/identity is substantiated. Unknown original attribution is recorded as unknown in provenance rather than guessed from ownership,user identity or permission. A user who edits content is included when that contribution is evidenced;authorization alone is not authorship. Later edits require independent resulting-byte assessment. Author is provenance,not automatic approval or responsibility for consequences of all later product changes.

CHANGELOG §Author is event-specific under policy §7.2. Solely assistant-executed events retain Codex — AI assistant. A jointly authored event lists actual contributors with the same separator. Earlier events are preserved;the cumulative document list is not copied retroactively into them.

The proposed batch changes only DF-DID-003 plus central tracking/evidence. Family and Component become Document Identity;Type Architecture remains appropriate for conceptual entities/relations. Proposed cumulative Author is **Arnaldo Figueira; Codex — AI assistant** under policy §7.1. Original contribution remains;actual later edit is recorded separately from authorization and CHANGELOG event authorship. No source author is added during this unchanged-source REVIEW. Proposed V2.0 R1 is subject to actual written compatibility assessment. Universal every-document existence/permanent-identity claims,mandatory-reference interpretation,unqualified model authority/immutability and guaranteed uniqueness/resolution/independence need normative reconciliation with qualified current owners. Cardinality correction changes what is required or allowed;it is assessed explicitly rather than treated as editorial. Family/links/attribution alone are compatible. Actual UPDATE must justify major/minor action and monotonic Revision;no ID/path/domain migration or actual allocation is performed.

#### 56.3.1. Entities and four relationship/cardinality contracts

| Received entity | Proposed subject/owner interpretation |
|---|---|
| Document | Applicable normalized logical document;physical representation,exact edition and pre-allocation subject distinguished |
| Document Identity | Same-document qualified Domain+Identifier allocation/preservation contract;mutable record facts separate |
| Identifier | Canonical representation under the actual domain contract,not the logical entity or current classification |
| Identity Domain | Explicit uniqueness/allocation/preservation scope with actual owner/rules;not bare-string global uniqueness |
| Identity Reference | Typed association to an intended qualified document/edition target;not another key or guaranteed live resolver |

| Received §6 relationship/cardinality | Proposed UPDATE interpretation | Remaining boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Document→Document Identity1:1 | One valid assigned identity for an applicable normalized document in its declared contract;pre-allocation and functional-file subjects separate | No universal document-existence permission or automatic DF allocation for every asset |
| Document Identity→Identifier1:1 | One canonical representation for an assigned key under governing domain rules;aliases/references distinct | No complete merged schema or arbitrary representation/domain change |
| Identity Domain→Document Identity1:N | Each assigned identity belongs to one domain;domain population may be empty absent an applicable owner minimum | Explicit direction/bounds/subject;no invented mandatory external-domain registry |
| Document Identity→Identity Reference1:N | Proposed selected conceptual incoming-reference multiplicity0..N,following DID-005's explicit optionality;each valid document-targeting reference has its intended qualified target | Original1:N contrast retained;local model change not yet applied;schema/Specification propagation pending |

No supplied clause justifies requiring an incoming reference merely for an assigned identity to exist. DID-005 §§4.3/5 explicitly permits zero or more. DID-006 §5.1 recognizes an Unassigned subject before allocation,requiring the document1:1 statement to be scoped rather than treated as universal existence denial. Domain membership versus domain population direction must be explicit. Numeric bounds are not silently inferred from an ambiguous N notation or applied to other entity domains.

DID-004 Identity Status uses Active/Archived/Deprecated;DID-006/014 state vocabulary is Unassigned/Assigned/Preserved/Retired. DID-014 §§7/8 requires Identity State/Canonical Reference under a schema differing from DID-005. The Model's five entities do not justify fabricating a mandatory merged state/reference field catalog. Stable key,pre-allocation/assignment/preservation/retirement record,document-edition Status and exact member facts remain distinct;full owner/schema/lifecycle cycles remain pending.

#### 56.3.2. Both view meanings and lifetime/representation evidence

The unnamed §3 view combines Document possession,Identity representation and six consumer/area labels. Preserve full received text/digest and declare selected conceptual associations;arrows/order do not establish required service routing or execution. Identity Domain and Reference subjects from the entity table/cardinality contracts need explicit treatment rather than an inferred completeness guarantee from the drawing.

The unnamed §7 chain preserves six received labels:Document Created,Identity Assigned,Document Evolves,Identity Preserved,Document Archived,Identity Retained. Those document events and preservation obligations are not a universal lifecycle-state graph,permission model or mandatory serial execution. DF defines contracts;DMS/tool/manual processes execute and CMS governs its own change scope. Source state/assignment disagreements remain visible.

Logical identity remains associated with the same document through ordinary evolution. Active use,retirement and retained historical associations/reservations differ;allocated/retired identities are not reassigned. Preserved reference interpretation does not guarantee a running resolver,retrievable content or publication. Necessary authorized representation/allocation migration first reconciles governing previous/new contracts with responsible owners and records old-new/continuity/alias/reservation/collision/history/edition/consumer/transition evidence. Historical snapshot selections remain pinned;no arbitrary reassignment or actual migration occurs here.

Domain+Identifier qualifies logical identity. Stable DF-SSS-NNN allocation series are independent of current Family/Type/path/Version/Revision and are not external-domain syntax requirements. Canonical identifiers and alternative aliases/representations follow actual intended-target rules. One canonical interpretation is a requirement,not demonstrated globally available resolution. Exact-edition targeting additionally requires coordinates/bytes/context;identity alone selects neither edition nor Baseline membership.

#### 56.3.3. Concern and model cooperation owners

The six concern and six model-association rows remain traceable. Governance is an area grouping Workflow,Policies,Reviews and Baselines,not a new component. Discovery includes Registry,Relationships,Knowledge Graph,Navigation and Search;its received three-name subset is incomplete as an area inventory. Metadata and Templates retain actual capability/domain roles.

Metadata represents descriptors while responsible semantic domains retain meaning. Dictionary defines,Schema organizes,Profile constrains and Instance supplies values under one effective Profile. MTD-003 §8 Schema-defines-Elements versus MTD-013 Dictionary definition/catalog responsibility remains pending owner/catalog/Profile reconciliation;no field or Profile is fabricated.

Relationships owns typed records between endpoints whose identity contracts follow their entity kinds;not all endpoints are documents. Knowledge Graph projects document nodes,other entities and typed records with source/edition provenance/freshness;edges do not receive normalized document IDs by implication. Registry lookup/catalog scope and exact Baseline member selection need actual context rather than universal routing or global network guarantees.

Required uses are consumer→provider contracts with explicit scope/source/interface under support order,same-Layer and prohibited-cycle rules. Documentary authority,concept association,projection,navigation and execution remain distinct. Both README DID→Metadata descriptors and DID-009 Metadata→DID/no consumer dependency claims remain explicit unresolved evidence. Different key/descriptor uses alone do not reconcile actual indispensable contracts or prove acyclicity;no edge is hidden by a changed label.

The component owns documentary identity semantics;the Model explains entities/relations/cardinalities under actual Architecture/Concepts and detailed Schema/Constraints/Dictionary/Specification roles. It is not the exclusive source for every identity concept. Authority follows applicable clauses/incorporation,not Type/title/Canonical status or presence. Conceptual technology neutrality permits explicit logical/canonicalization/reference/integration requirements;actual adapters/executors and observations determine conformance. Controlled extension assesses compatible meaning,owners/consumers/dependencies and transition evidence rather than guaranteeing harmless independence.

Retain the six §9.4 subjects:storage technologies,repositories,programming languages,serialization formats,communication protocols and implementation frameworks. Technology neutrality is a scoped AP-003 application under legitimate logical/interface/adapter requirements,not universal compatibility or complete independence from actual integration contracts. No characteristic/property/AP ID is fabricated and no full24-principle implemented-conformity result is inferred.

Model qualities and normative obligations remain separate from actual achieved evidence. Rules,exact artifacts/editions/context,checks,observations,executor,coverage and unresolved conditions determine results. Local correction/validation confers neither approval/publication/Baseline nor full peer/global implementation.
